| `APS5_NO_HOST_F32_NANS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | skips the startup f32 NaN probe and keeps the selected NaN results of the f32 add, subtract, multiply and multiply-add forms in every shader |
| `APS5_NO_HOST_IMPORT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | disables importing guest memory, so address-based shaders copy instead of using it in place |
| `APS5_NO_HOST_INTERPOLATION_FIRST` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | on RADV, reads pixel shader inputs through VK_KHR_fragment_shader_barycentric again instead of trying host interpolation first |
| `APS5_NO_HTILE_FIXUP_CARRY` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | a draw's depth target whose HTILE (single-sample, no stencil) has pending GPU writes from the HTILE ZMASK fixup dispatch resolves the pending fills or reads the HTILE, instead of keeping the answer the HTILE gave before those fixups when each fixup ran while that answer was current; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_HW_F32_FLUSH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | skips the startup f32 denormal flush probe and keeps the emulated flush in every shader |
| `APS5_NO_HW_F32_PRESERVE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | skips the startup f32 denormal preserve probe and stops requesting DenormPreserve for 32-bit floats, so the device keeps its default f32 denormal handling |
| `APS5_NO_IDLE_STORE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Queues/QueueWorker.cpp` |  | stops queue 0's worker from storing pending storage-image results after the queues stay idle for 50 ms, so they stay pending until a reader, label or flush stores them; queue 0 still waits for work while they are pending |
| `APS5_NO_IMAGE_PAGE_CACHE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/GuestMemory.cpp` |  | leaves the main guest image's page-protection table uncached |
| `APS5_NO_IMAGE_VIEW_MIN_LOD` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | leaves VK_EXT_image_view_min_lod disabled, so a T#'s minimum LOD is applied in the shader where shaderResourceMinLod allows it and graphics pipeline libraries are off |
| `APS5_NO_INDIRECT_DRAW` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Pm4.cpp` |  | rejects indirect draw packets at submission validation |
| `APS5_NO_INPUT_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with APS5_LATE_MS set, runs the in-place checks (whether a vertex or index range can be bound in place from a host import) for every draw, instead of taking the thread's answer for the same range, alignment and kind in the same open batch while nothing those checks read has moved (pending writes, labels, stores, storage results, host imports, guest mappings); APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_INPUT_MIRRORS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` |  | binds a reused vertex or index snapshot from host memory instead of copying it to a device-local mirror at its first reuse |
| `APS5_NO_INTERVAL_COLLECT_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | no effect: a thread's map of the write collects it completed in the current collect epoch always merges the epoch's walks under the same serials and answers sub-ranges and ranges that adjoining walks cover together; the switch stays in the registry so APS5_AB lists that name it keep parsing |
| `APS5_NO_JOIN_WAW_CHECK` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` |  | lets a contiguous store join the pending one without the write-after-write check |
| `APS5_NO_KEEP_NEW_STORAGE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | does not keep a newly created storage image alive with the open batch; such an image evicted before the batch completes can hand its memory to another image through the buffer pool while the batch still writes it |
| `APS5_NO_KEYS_FILL_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/DccMetadata.cpp` |  | records no memo of GPU-side DCC key fills |
| `APS5_NO_KEYS_REFILL` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | keeps a covered image's uploaded keys as they were instead of marking them uncompressed after a matching key fill |
| `APS5_NO_KEYS_RESCAN` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | reuses the DCC keys scanned before the lookup's refresh instead of discarding them for a rescan |
| `APS5_NO_KEY_FAST_PATH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/DccMetadata.cpp` |  | scans a surface's DCC keys on every call instead of using and storing a proof |
| `APS5_NO_LABEL_BATCHING` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/DeferredLabels.cpp` |  | turns off every label-batching behaviour, the label runs, queued labels and batched submit |
| `APS5_NO_LABEL_BATCH_SUBMIT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/DeferredLabels.cpp` |  | disables batched label submission |
| `APS5_NO_LABEL_PACKET_FLUSH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Packets/PacketFlush.cpp` |  | disables the label flush between packets, which runs only when batched label submission is off (`APS5_NO_LABEL_BATCH_SUBMIT` or `APS5_NO_LABEL_BATCHING`) |
| `APS5_NO_LABEL_READBACK_STORE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | leaves pending linear and small storage images on the GPU at a label instead of storing them before it |
| `APS5_NO_LABEL_READ_TRAP` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | on macOS, stores pending linear and small storage images before a label instead of trapping reads of their pages until something stores them |
| `APS5_NO_LABEL_RUNS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` |  | records every store in a run of its own between a barrier pair |
| `APS5_NO_LABEL_SHORTCUT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/WaitMemory.cpp` |  | disables the pending-label table shortcut in a memory wait |
| `APS5_NO_LAST_FRAGMENT_OUTPUTS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | asks the shared validation memo for every draw instead of serving a repeat of the thread's last fragment-output key |
| `APS5_NO_LAYER_CLEAR` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | tracks every surface as one layer instead of per array layer |
| `APS5_NO_LAYER_REFUSAL_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | re-checks a layer whose surface check failed at its current generation on every fill |
| `APS5_NO_LAYOUT_CACHE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | builds descriptor set layouts fresh instead of through the descriptor cache |
| `APS5_NO_LEAN_RUNS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with APS5_LATE_MS set, the draw committer takes the GPU lock for each queued draw on its own, instead of keeping one hold across a run of up to 32 queued draws, which ends at a non-draw item, an empty queue, a failure or a thread waiting for the lock or the pipeline; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_LEASE_MIRROR` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | restores copies instead of image mirrors for address-based builds |
| `APS5_NO_LIBRARY_PREBUILD` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Shaders/ShaderRegistry.cpp` |  | disables prebuilding registration-form shader libraries (only 1 disables it) |
| `APS5_NO_LINEAR_IMPORT_RETILE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | retiles a whole-layer write-back of a linear storage image into device scratch and copies its rows into the host import, instead of retiling it into the import directly (which is done only when the import is in a device-local memory heap, as on unified memory) |
| `APS5_NO_LIST_RESTART` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | leaves `VK_EXT_primitive_topology_list_restart` off, so indexed list draws with primitive restart compact their indices on the CPU |
| `APS5_NO_LOCK_PROFILE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/GuestMemory.cpp` |  | turns off GpuMutex wait profiling under APS5_PROFILE_DRAW |
| `APS5_NO_MEMO_PENDING_COLLECT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | walks a storage image's units again when its results become pending, instead of using a walk made after the caller's ordering point; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_MESH_INLINE_FUNCTIONS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | keeps a mesh program's helper functions `DontInline` on NVIDIA's proprietary driver instead of asking the driver to inline every function |
| `APS5_NO_MESH_WINDOW_BUFFER` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | keeps a mesh program's relative-addressing window in function arrays on NVIDIA's proprietary driver instead of moving it to a storage buffer |
| `APS5_NO_MOVED_BUFFERS_IN_PLACE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | rebuilds the shader resources of a draw whose read-only buffer moved into a range that recorded GPU work still writes, instead of reading the buffer through its host import on the cached set |
| `APS5_NO_MOVED_BUFFER_TEMPLATES` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | keys the full descriptor words of read-only guest buffers instead of allowing a hit when they move |
| `APS5_NO_OBSERVATION_GUARD` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Memory/WriteEvidence.cpp` |  | observes every validated range, not only the ranges whose flush hook waited |
| `APS5_NO_OPPORTUNISTIC_REAP` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | skips retiring already-finished batches before a submit |
| `APS5_NO_ORDERED_WAITS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/WaitStatistics.cpp` |  | bumps the collect epoch at every memory wait instead of skipping a wait on an unchanged flag an earlier wait already ordered |
| `APS5_NO_OWNED_DRAW_INPUTS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | disables token-based reuse of untracked vertex/index copies; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_IMPORT_SEGMENT_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | recomputes a storage image's host-import segments at every refresh instead of reusing them while the guest mapping generation and the import table are unchanged; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_OWN_IMAGE_REFRESH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | lets a Pending failure of the fast proof take the full walk instead of the own-object refresh |
| `APS5_NO_OWN_STORE_NOTES` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | makes FlushPending wait for a storage image's own earlier GPU-direct write-back when the image is not a drawn target, as before |
| `APS5_NO_PARALLEL_MIRROR_REFRESH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | disables the mirror-refresh thread pool, so mirror refreshes run on the calling thread |
| `APS5_NO_PASS_ACCESS_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with APS5_LATE_MS set, derives every draw's pass access (its template's device reads, hazard writes, write-only ranges, storage images and whether it reads a target) anew, instead of reusing it when the same template draws into the same targets in the same render pass (address-based and address-table builds always derive it); APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_PASS_ATTACHMENT_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | looks up every draw's color and depth attachments anew instead of taking the previous draw's while the open render pass, its targets, the proof serials and the images are unchanged; it is consulted before the target memo (`APS5_NO_TARGET_MEMO`), which a draw it serves leaves untouched; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_PATTERN_FILL_HLE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Memory/BufferFill.cpp` |  | disables the dword-pattern fill HLE |
| `APS5_NO_PAUSE_SPIN` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/WaitMemory.cpp` |  | disables the CPU pause-spin in a blocked memory wait |
| `APS5_NO_PENDING_HTILE_FILLS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/DepthSurface.cpp` |  | reads the HTILE words of a depth target bind even when recorded fills are pending over them, instead of working the words out from the fills |
| `APS5_NO_PERF_FRONTEND_PAIR` | presence | `core/shader/recompiler/Recompiler.cpp` |  | disables the recompiler's frontend-pair reuse; the shader disk cache treats it as output-neutral |
| `APS5_NO_PERF_LAYOUT_PAIR` | presence | `core/shader/recompiler/Recompiler.cpp` |  | disables the recompiler's layout-pair reuse; the shader disk cache treats it as output-neutral |
| `APS5_NO_PIPELINED_INDIRECT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Draw/Draw.cpp` |  | drains the draw pipeline at every CPU-path indirect draw instead of queuing it behind the pipeline |
| `APS5_NO_PIPELINE_CACHE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Pipeline.cpp` |  | builds a pipeline per draw instead of reusing cached ones |
| `APS5_NO_PIPELINE_JOURNAL` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/PipelineJournal.cpp` |  | keeps the pipeline journal off; 0 leaves it on, and it is also off while the shader disk cache is disabled |
| `APS5_NO_PIPELINE_LTO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/PipelineLibrary.cpp` |  | disables background link-time optimization of graphics pipeline libraries |
| `APS5_NO_PLAN_CLEAN_PROOFS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | the committer half of APS5_STAGE_PLANS draws proves each planned draw's template in full again instead of keeping a clean-currency proof (APS5_CLEAN_CURRENCY's rule, which planned draws take without that switch); the `[stage-plans]` line counts those proofs as proved in full with clean proofs off; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_POOL_CACHE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | allocates descriptor pools fresh instead of through the descriptor cache |
| `APS5_NO_PRECOLLECT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | disables the stage-A precollect pass |
| `APS5_NO_PREPARED_FIND` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | looks the resource-cache key up again instead of using the object stage A found |
| `APS5_NO_PRESENT_EPOCH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | keeps the presenter at collect epoch 0 instead of adopting the newest epoch a worker started |
| `APS5_NO_PRESYNC` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | disables the stage-A pre-sync of pending writes |
| `APS5_NO_PROC_TABLE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | resolves the device's Vulkan entry points per call instead of filling the function table once |
| `APS5_NO_QUEUED_DRAW_READ_ORDER` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Draw/DrawPipeline.cpp` |  | stops queued draws and DMA copies from holding back write-backs over the guest memory they read at commit |
| `APS5_NO_QUEUED_LABELS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/DeferredLabels.cpp` |  | disables the queued-label table |
| `APS5_NO_RANGED_DISPATCH_BREAKS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with APS5_LATE_MS set, a direct dispatch whose writes are all known breaks every template proof through its GPU lock, instead of breaking only the proofs over its writes and the labels it records (APS5_NO_RANGE_PROOFS turns that off too); APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_RANGE_PROOFS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with APS5_LATE_MS set, a template's epoch proof holds only while no proof was broken since it was taken, instead of also across breaks whose logged guest ranges miss the template's own; the GPU locks the flush hook, waits, flushes, the presenter and lock tries take outside the proof threads then break every proof too, as do the draw pipeline's non-draw items and every dispatch; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_READS_OVERLAP_WALK` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | builds the list of in-place reads for each read-overlap test instead of walking the regions to the first overlap |
| `APS5_NO_READ_ONLY_LEASE_SKIP` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | counts the lease of an address-based build whose shaders never store through BDA as a pending writer of its leased range instead of only a reader |
| `APS5_NO_RECENT_IMPORTS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | looks every host import up in the import map instead of first checking the last four found while the map is unchanged |
| `APS5_NO_RECORDED_CLEAR` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | waits for a storage image's fast clear in a batch of its own instead of recording it |
| `APS5_NO_RECORDER_SYNC_DRAWS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | gives the draws the recorder waits for their own batch instead of recording them with the wait |
| `APS5_NO_RECORD_COPIED_DRAWS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | waits for copied-write draws at once instead of listing their write-back for later |
| `APS5_NO_RECT_INDIRECT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Draw/IndirectDraw.cpp` |  | sends an indirect rectangle-list draw down the CPU path instead of the GPU-side path |
| `APS5_NO_REFRESH_GPU_ORDERED` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | waits on the CPU for the pending writers a storage refresh flushes and compares the guest bytes, instead of re-uploading the layers from the import behind the write-backs |
| `APS5_NO_RESIDENT_LINEAR` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | drops the residency of linear color targets, so a target whose address has a host import, or whose import a draw would make, is copied in and out again |
| `APS5_NO_RESIDENT_PRESENT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | always presents through guest memory instead of the resident image |
| `APS5_NO_RESIDENT_TARGETS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | copies every render target in and out again instead of keeping it resident |
| `APS5_NO_RESOURCE_CACHE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | disables the shared resource cache, so every dispatch builds its resources fresh |
| `APS5_NO_REWATCH_UNDER_IMAGES` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | keeps a settled Windows import compared while a live storage image overlaps it, instead of returning it to the write watch and proving the image's rewatched blocks unchanged by comparison (Windows only) |
| `APS5_NO_RUN_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | walks the lease for a host import's run of ranges on every lookup instead of reusing the bounds found for the same lease snapshot and import window block |
| `APS5_NO_SAMPLED_FROM_STORAGE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | keeps every sampled texture a CPU snapshot instead of viewing the surface's storage image |
| `APS5_NO_SAMPLED_IMPORT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | keeps a sampled uncompressed surface with a pending GPU write a CPU snapshot instead of uploading it on the GPU from its host import |
| `APS5_NO_SAMPLER_CACHE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | creates a sampler per binding instead of caching them |
| `APS5_NO_SEPARATE_QUEUED_LABELS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` |  | lets a queued label enter the recorded table and replace a recorded entry of its dword |
| `APS5_NO_SETTLED_INDIRECT_READ` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | keeps an indirect draw whose records no unfinished batch writes on the GPU-side path instead of reading the records on the CPU |
| `APS5_NO_SHAPE_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | looks up every draw's resident targets and pipeline anew instead of repeating the ones an earlier draw of the same register shape used |
| `APS5_NO_SHARED_COLLECT_WALKS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | walks a range again for every queue worker and every ordering point instead of reusing a walk another thread made after the caller's ordering point (a submission dates from the game's submit call, a wait on a label another queue stored from that queue's point); APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_SHARED_FAULT_SLOTS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/BdaResources.cpp` |  | gives every BDA resource object its own full-size fault buffer for its whole life instead of a slot from the device's shared fault chunks that an idle cached object gives back (a 32-byte slot when its programs can write only the fault report) |
| `APS5_NO_SHARED_GDS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Pm4.cpp` |  | keeps the GDS in a private guest mapping, which the GPU cannot import, instead of anonymous shared memory on an integrated GPU with host imports; DMA_DATA into the GDS behind GPU writes to it then drains, and DMA_DATA out of it is read on the CPU |
| `APS5_NO_SINGLE_PASS_COLLECT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/GuestMemory.cpp` |  | walks the written pages once without clearing them and, when any were written, a second time clearing them, instead of one cleared pass |
| `APS5_NO_SITE_SAMPLING` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Memory/WriteEvidence.cpp` |  | disables per-site sampling of write evidence |
| `APS5_NO_SIZE_ONLY_SURFACE_BYTES` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | sizes a texture for its byte count by building the whole surface description, with its mip chain on the heap, instead of the size-only path that keeps a thin chain on the stack; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_SNAPSHOT_CHECK` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | skips the live compare that proves a captured snapshot still equals the guest bytes |
| `APS5_NO_SORTED_SNAPSHOT_LOOKUP` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | scans the snapshot's regions in AddSnapshot instead of searching them |
| `APS5_NO_SPACE_PATCH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | rebuilds the cached address space after an import change instead of patching its in-place regions to the current imports |
| `APS5_NO_SPACE_REUSE_RECORDS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | drops the sampled-texture validation records of a read-only address-based draw build that its cached address space can serve again, as every other BDA build does, so each reuse of it proves its textures by the full walk; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_SPACE_STAMP_ONCE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | stamps every direct import of a cached address space on each hit instead of once per space |
| `APS5_NO_SPANNED_SNAPSHOT_LOADS` | presence | `core/shader/recompiler/SpirvBackend/src/SpirvEmitter.cpp` |  | stops the spanned-wave uniformity check from treating a load from the `NonWritable` flattened SRT buffer at a uniform index as unwritten memory, so those spanned programs fall back to two lanes per invocation again (in the shader disk cache key when set) |
| `APS5_NO_SPANNED_WAVE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | keeps single-wave wave64 compute programs at two guest lanes per invocation instead of spanning full host subgroups |
| `APS5_NO_SPARSE_HEAP_MIRROR` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | disables sparse heap mirrors, mirroring only whole heap ranges |
| `APS5_NO_SPARSE_TEXTURE_LEVELS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | gives a sampled texture device memory for every level, also for the levels above its T#'s minimum LOD whose guest memory is not mapped, instead of leaving them unbound in a sparse image |
| `APS5_NO_STAGE_A_IMAGES` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | leaves every image element to the stage-B full lookup instead of a stage-A record |
| `APS5_NO_STAGE_DATA_SIDE_CARS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with APS5_LATE_MS set, a stage that matched its draw-cache variant except for data words records a copy of the compiled stage with the words patched in, so its draw gets no draw recipe, and a relocated stage is patched into a copy too, instead of keeping the variant's own compiled stage with the patched words beside it (only stages without a heap template, ring template or GPU-resolved images take side cars); APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_STATE_SUPPRESSION` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` |  | records every graphics pipeline bind and dynamic state command instead of skipping those the command buffer already holds |
| `APS5_NO_STORAGE_DEDUPE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | looks each identical consecutive storage descriptor up, and refreshes its image, separately |
| `APS5_NO_STORED_KNOWN_VALUES` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Memory/WriteEvidence.cpp` |  | makes a capture wait for the GPU over a store it recorded instead of reading the recorded bytes |
| `APS5_NO_SYNC_SITES` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` |  | leaves only the per-source and per-thread sync counts, with no per-site attribution |
| `APS5_NO_SYNC_THROUGH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` |  | makes every pending-write sync a full Sync instead of the targeted unlocked wait |
| `APS5_NO_TABLE_REPLAY` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | makes a reused bound image table rerun each element's checks instead of replaying its recorded bind outcomes |
| `APS5_NO_TABLE_REUSE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | disables reusing a materialized image table |
| `APS5_NO_TARGET_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | resolves every draw's color and depth targets anew instead of repeating the previous draw's while nothing they depend on changed; a runtime switch, so `APS5_AB` can toggle it: switched on, the next draw drops the memo, and a draw after it is switched off again starts a new one |
| `APS5_NO_TARGET_MEMO_OVERLAP_CACHE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | recomputes, on every target memo hit, whether each color target overlaps the draw's depth planes, instead of remembering the answer for the depth footprint the colors were proved against; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_TARGET_WRITEBACK` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | skips writing render targets back to guest memory after the draw |
| `APS5_NO_TEMPLATE_DATA_REFRESH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | keys the ShaderData and FlattenedSrt words into compute keys instead of refreshing a template's data buffers |
| `APS5_NO_TEXTURE_CACHE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | builds every texture and storage-image object fresh instead of caching it |
| `APS5_NO_TEXTURE_HASH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | finds texture-cache entries by scanning the list instead of the hash index |
| `APS5_NO_TEXTURE_PROOF_STAMPS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with APS5_LATE_MS set, a template's fast proof checks every texture and storage image itself and stamps none, instead of taking as proved an object another template's fast proof stamped under the same collect epoch and serials; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_NO_TIMELINE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | leaves timeline semaphores off, so drains wait under the mutex |
| `APS5_NO_UNIT_SHADOW` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/UnitShadow.cpp` |  | makes every unit shadow primitive inert |
| `APS5_NO_UNLOCKED_DEVICE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Dispatch/Dispatch.cpp` |  | loads the device and runs a dispatch under the GPU mutex instead of using an unlocked device |
| `APS5_NO_UNLOCKED_DRAIN` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Packets/PacketMemory.cpp` |  | keeps the GPU mutex held while draining a packet instead of the unlocked drain |
| `APS5_NO_UNLOCKED_VALIDATE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Dispatch/DispatchLookup.cpp` |  | keeps the dispatch-cache mutex held while validating a cache entry |
| `APS5_NO_UNREGISTERED_DROP` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Texture.cpp` |  | stores a storage image's pending results even when its memory is no longer registered, instead of dropping them |
| `APS5_NO_UNTRACKED_REPROOF` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp` |  | compares a snapshot over untracked memory without a hint on every lookup and sends a template whose stamp moved to the full walk, instead of proving the snapshot again once per collect epoch |
| `APS5_NO_VALIDATE_CACHE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | validates every draw instead of serving the validation memo |
| `APS5_NO_VALIDATE_SKIP` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Memory/WriteEvidence.cpp` |  | syncs every pending write instead of skipping validation on written-element evidence |
| `APS5_NO_VOUCHED_VALIDATION` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Dispatch/VariantValidation.cpp` |  | validates a cached draw or dispatch through its per-run checks instead of its table block's vouch |
| `APS5_NO_WAIT_OVERLAP_SUBMIT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/WaitMemory.cpp` |  | disables the unlocked wait that submits recorded work while overlapping writes are open |
| `APS5_NO_WORDWISE_CAPTURE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/ShaderMemory.cpp` |  | fetches every captured page whole instead of reading pending pages word by word |
| `APS5_NO_WORKER_AFFINITY` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Queues/WorkerAffinity.cpp` |  | leaves the queue workers and presenter unpinned (affinity mask 0) |
| `APS5_NO_WRITER_KEYED_EVIDENCE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Memory/WriteEvidence.cpp` |  | records written-element evidence without keying it by writer |
| `APS5_NO_WRITE_EVIDENCE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Memory/WriteEvidence.cpp` |  | disables the written-element evidence that lets a read skip the flush hook |
| `APS5_NO_WRITE_ONLY_PASSES` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | counts a buffer a draw only stores to as read and written, so the next draw storing to it begins a new render pass |
| `APS5_NO_WRITTEN_SHADOW` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/GuestBufferMemory.cpp` |  | binds written staging elements in place instead of through the written shadow |

| `APS5_OWN_DRAW_SETS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | a template keeps up to 32 per set index of the per-draw descriptor sets it wrote in full, taken back when the batch that used them completes, and a draw that takes one writes only its buffer descriptors (its image and sampler descriptors are the template's own); with the switch off sets go back to the descriptor cache as before and a template's kept sets go back to it at the template's next draw; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_PACKET_EPOCH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/WaitStatistics.cpp` |  | makes queue workers bump the collect epoch before every packet |
| `APS5_PIPELINED_DRAWS` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Draw/DrawPipeline.cpp` | 32 | depth of the queue-0 draw pipeline: 0 records draws inline, 1 selects a depth of 8, and any other value is the depth capped at 256 |
| `APS5_PIPELINED_GPU_INDIRECT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with `APS5_HANDOFF_GPU_INDIRECT`, lets the queue worker hand indirect draws to the draw finisher; an indirect draw whose arguments the GPU reads is committed through the draw pipeline with its argument and count ranges as queued reads whether this is set or not (`APS5_NO_PIPELINED_INDIRECT` turns that off); APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_CACHE_STATS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/PipelineCache.cpp` |  | every pipeline cache save that writes the file prints a `[pipeline-cache] save stats` line: KiB written, how long pipeline creation was held off by the save's snapshots, the copy out of a cache merged with the file, and the whole save; read once |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_DISPATCH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | records an eligible dispatch as an item in the queue-0 pipeline instead of running it inline; an empty value or one starting with `0` leaves it off; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_DISPATCH_MISSES` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with APS5_PIPELINE_DISPATCH, a dispatch that misses the dispatch cache, or whose cached variant has no recipe yet, is also queued as a commit item when the reads it captured are clear of queued writes and its writes are bounded: it captures with the draw pipeline busy, and the committer prepares and dispatches it and builds the recipe; HTILE clears and fix-ups, key fills and depth plane clears still drain; the `[dispatch-item]` line counts the items queued after a miss and without a recipe |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_DRAIN_DUMPS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Packets/PacketMemory.cpp` |  | drains the draw pipeline at every queue-0 `DUMP_CONST_RAM` instead of queuing its store |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_DRAIN_FILLS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Memory/BufferFill.cpp` |  | runs queue-0 buffer fills through the draw pipeline to keep their order; any value stores them in place instead |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_DRAIN_LABELS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Packets/PacketExecution.cpp` |  | answers a queue-0 wait from the draw pipeline's own pending labels while it is busy; any value drains the pipeline at the wait |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_DRAIN_SAMPLE_DUMPS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Packets/PacketMemory.cpp` |  | queues a queue-0 occlusion counter dump in the draw pipeline behind the draws before it; any value drains the pipeline at the dump |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_DRAIN_SUBMISSIONS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Packets/PacketExecution.cpp` |  | lets a submission end without submitting its recorded work, so it joins a later submit with the pipeline's queue; any value drains the pipeline and submits at the end of the submission |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_JOURNAL_COMPUTE_ENTRIES` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/PipelineJournal.cpp` | 1024 | number of compute pipelines a replay builds at most, the last recorded, counted apart from the graphics ones |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_JOURNAL_ENTRIES` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/PipelineJournal.cpp` | 4096 | number of graphics pipelines a replay builds at most, the last recorded; a log line names how many of each kind were left out |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_JOURNAL_MAX_MIB` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/PipelineJournal.cpp` | 1024 | size cap in MiB on the pipeline journal; the file is rewritten down to three quarters of the cap when it is reached, keeping the newest pipelines and the modules they name and leaving room for later records |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_JOURNAL_THREADS` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/PipelineJournal.cpp` |  | threads that replay the journal's pipelines, 0 disables the replay; unset uses half the hardware threads, at least one |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_STATS` | string | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | captures and prints driver statistics for each new pipeline, requiring `VK_KHR_pipeline_executable_properties`; `1` enables; graphics reports include `[pipeline-source]` Vulkan stage bit, module variant, specialized pipeline variant and stable pipeline identity; disables graphics pipeline libraries, so these counters do not describe the normal GPL/LTO shaders |
| `APS5_PIPELINE_STORE_ENTRIES` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Pipeline.cpp` | adaptive | fixes the LRU bound on the entries of the pipeline store, which otherwise starts at 1024 and doubles up to 4096 for a journal replay that does not fit and while evicted pipelines keep coming back; an entry is evicted only once no recorded draw holds it |
| `APS5_PLAIN_ALPHA_FALLBACK` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/State.cpp` |  | draws a dithered alpha-to-coverage state (DB_ALPHA_TO_MASK 0x18701) whose sample configuration has no mask table with plain alpha-to-coverage instead of ending the run: coverage switches at half alpha in every pixel instead of following the dither pattern; the first such states print an `[alpha-to-coverage]` line with their PA_SC_AA_CONFIG and DB_EQAA words whether the switch is set or not |
| `APS5_PM4_RING` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Queues/SubmissionRing.cpp` | 16 | number of submissions the PM4 ring keeps for dumps, from 0 to 4096; 0 disables the ring |
| `APS5_POISON_VERTEX_PREFIX` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | fills the bytes of a copied vertex range below its lowest fetched vertex with 0x7f instead of leaving them unset, so a draw that reads them shows in its frames |
| `APS5_POLL_REAP` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/WaitMemory.cpp` |  | reaps completions on every poll tick |
| `APS5_POLL_TRY_EACH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/WaitMemory.cpp` |  | tries the poll service on every tick instead of at the poll interval |
| `APS5_PREBUILD_THREADS` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Shaders/ShaderRegistry.cpp` |  | threads in the pool that prebuilds registration-form shader libraries and compute pipelines; unset uses half the hardware threads, at least two. Compute pipeline prebuilds, which wait for `APS5_BACKGROUND_COMPILE_KIB`, run on at most half of them (at least one) |
| `APS5_PRECISE_LOOKUP_DRAIN` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | the queue worker drains the draw finisher before a draw cache lookup only for pending draws of the same key or shape that will insert into the cache (no entry or no full hit at their lookup), not for pending full hits; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_PRESENT_POLL_FENCE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/VulkanDevice.cpp` |  | polls the present fence every 50 us instead of blocking on it |
| `APS5_PROBE_DISPATCH` | string | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Dispatch/Dispatch.cpp` |  | probes one dispatch at a hex program address (optionally `:index`) with debug output |
| `APS5_PROFILE_DRAW` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Dispatch/Dispatch.cpp` |  | prints the per-phase driver and draw timing totals |
| `APS5_PRECISE_SETTLE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | the draw finisher's settle test of the ranges a job read (stage walks, decode reads, buffers, inputs) asks the write tracker's page and store-piece stamps when the 64 KiB block of a range carries a stamp newer than the job (`GuestMemory::UnchangedSince`) instead of refusing the job, so a write to another page of the block no longer sends the draw to the committer's own preparation; the `[direct-unsettled]` line counts the ranges settled that way; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_PROFILE_GPU` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` |  | records the GPU time of recorded work by key from batch timestamps |
| `APS5_PROFILE_GPU_DRAWS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Draw/Draw.cpp`, `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | with APS5_PROFILE_GPU, records the GPU time of each recorded draw by its fragment program's variant id and prints a [drawvariant] line with the code address behind each variant id once (draws that do not use a recipe only; the [gputime] line labels each `draw 0x<variant>`, the id [drawvariant] prints, and the ranges share the batch's query cap) |
| `APS5_PROFILE_TARGET_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Draw.cpp` |  | prints one `[target-memo]` line per 10 s summed over every thread: target memo lookups, hits and accepts, depth hits, refusals by reason (invalid, thread changed, descriptor, resident gone or evicted, depth overlap, metadata, each proof field that moved, post-depth invalidation, admission, recipe draws), memo builds and `APS5_VERIFY_TARGET_MEMO` checks; the counts are only kept while this switch or `APS5_PROFILE_DRAW` is set, so the memo hit path costs nothing without them |
| `APS5_PUSH_MEMCPY` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | a draw's push constant block (pushDrawConstants and the stencil replay) is assembled with one memcpy per stage and a dword overlap mask instead of a per-byte loop over a 256-entry occupancy array; the same checks fail on the same inputs; dispatches and recipes keep the byte loop; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_READONLY_DEPTH_BOUNDS_COMPARE` | string | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Draw/DrawDecode.cpp` | 0 | `1` enables a read-only GREATER_OR_EQUAL comparison at viewport depth 1 for eligible single-sample D32S8 draws with native depth bounds; read once per process; unset or `0` keeps the normal path |
| `APS5_REAP_EACH_PACKET` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Synchronization/DeferredLabels.cpp` |  | reaps deferred labels after every packet |
| `APS5_REARM_QUIET` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with resident arming on, a resident run the guest wrote after it was armed is armed again once 8 clearing collects in a row found it unwritten, at most once per 50 ms per run, instead of staying on GetWriteWatch for good; the `[arming]` line (APS5_STAGE_STATS) counts the collects of such runs, the unwritten ones and the runs armed again; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_RECYCLE_COMMAND_BUFFERS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | the command buffer of a batch whose fence has signaled is kept (up to 16) and begun again for a later batch (the pool's implicit reset) instead of being freed and a new one allocated; a discarded batch, a failed begin and a failed fence wait still free theirs, and with the switch off the kept ones are freed at the next batch release; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_REFUSED_COLLECT_MEMO` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | a memoized write collect whose walk the guest arena refused (it answers 0, untracked) is remembered for the collect epoch, so later collects of the same page range in the epoch answer 0 without walking again; the memo drops with the epoch, the unwatch serial, the allocation generation and the forget serial, as the completed-walk memo does; the `[collect-walks]` line of APS5_PIPELINE_STATS counts complete and refused walks, the bytes walked, the answers taken from the memo and the ten most refused ranges; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_REGISTRATION_PREPARE_COMMIT_GB` | number | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Shaders/ShaderRegistry.cpp` | half of physical RAM | stops preparing newly registered shaders ahead of use when process-private memory reaches this GiB budget; `0` disables registration-time preparation |
| `APS5_RELEASE_ON_UNLOCK` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` |  | destroys deferred batches on the unlocking thread instead of a release thread |
| `APS5_RELEASE_QUEUE_MAX` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` | 256 | maximum deferred batches handed to the release thread before a thread destroys its own |
| `APS5_RELEASE_UNDER_LOCK` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/Recorder.cpp` |  | destroys deferred batches in finish() under the lock instead of on a release thread |
| `APS5_RELOCATION_DRAIN` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Draw/DrawRelocate.cpp` |  | drains the draw finisher before a draw whose previous draw missed the draw cache and learns that draw's relocation on the worker, instead of learning it on the thread that inserts the draw cache entry |
| `APS5_REPLAY_BIND` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | with APS5_REPLAY_HEAP_STAGE, a heap template checks its guest buffers against depth and multisample guards and takes its fault slot once per draw instead of again when the heaps are bound, and a replayed stage patches its changed user data words into the words an addressed-buffer overlay starts from, so an overlay after a replay carries the draw's constants; a replayed stage whose shader data the overlay rewrites writes no span of its own (the next draw without an overlay writes one), and the draw's snapshot buffers are kept once per batch instead of keeping the draw's bindings object; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_REPLAY_CENSUS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/HeapReplay.cpp` |  | counts what the template hit replay could cover and prints a `[replay-census]` line every 10 s and at each APS5_AB phase event: heap template hits that the heap stage replay would take and the first reason that refuses the others, how each heap stage refresh differs from the stage's previous inputs (same, only 1, 2-4, 5-16 or 17+ user data dwords outside the template's patched dwords, words at or above the user data, a patched dword, slots or size, and separately words that changed only under the user data), whether the flattened SRT changed and where the shader data words came from, how many unchanged stage data blocks and SRTs still lie in an open ring chunk, how many entries of each per-draw ring descriptor run differ from the template's previous run, the snapshots per heap template hit (moved, unmoved, bytes, unwritten-memo hits, draw uploads and large snapshots), the recorded hits that continue the pass after a draw of the same template and with its pipeline and framebuffer, how often the guest allocations generation, the host import set and the heap slot serial moved between hits of a template, and the chunks other than the current one that are still open at each submit; an empty value or one starting with `0` leaves it off |
| `APS5_REPLAY_HEAP_STAGE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | template hits of descriptor heap draws on the committer refresh their heap stages after the snapshot selection from a per-template record instead of in full at resolve time: changed user data dwords are patched into the stage data's CPU copy after its new span is allocated, the slot key is not rebuilt while the slot serial or generations hold, an unchanged span is kept without a hold while no submit happened since it was proved, and the template's descriptor run is built only for draws without an overlay (a predicted overlay the draw does not get builds it before the pass); any refresh outside the replay, a slot, size, word or patched-dword change, a new program or heap, a switch flip and a submit before the pass send the draw to today's refresh, a submit inside the replay's own refreshes re-seeds the record from today's refresh, and a fallback refresh is repeated until no submit lands in it; with APS5_VERIFY_GPU_HEAP each replayed stage data block is also rebuilt and compared; an empty value or one starting with `0` leaves it off; APS5_AB can toggle it |
| `APS5_RESIDENCY_CENSUS` | flag | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ResidencyCensus.cpp` | 0 | census of the copies between guest memory and the GPU for choosing the GPU residency steps, for a measurement boot of its own (at least 130 s in game, never a speed measurement): no decision of the driver and no byte it copies changes, and no GPU work is added except the sampled line census of APS5_RESIDENCY_CENSUS_LINES; the census's own subscriptions can change the order in which a wide bump visits the write index's blocks, and with it the point at which another write subscription's recorded bumps overflow and its proof falls back to its full check. Every device-local staged region (keyed by its exact [begin, end)) gets a write subscription of its own that counts the write index's bumps over it by source, and the driver stamps by the code that made them (staged copy-back marks with the region's id, in-place marks and dispatches with their program address, completion re-stamps, image write-backs with their base address, and the recorder's other writer tags). Every 10 s, `[residency-census]` lines: `period`; per region (largest first, at most 32) a `region` line (uses and uses per present, reused and new builds, the builds and programs that used it, the overlapping regions, and whether a copy kept on the GPU since the previous use would still have been valid: `step1Hits` with the first failing condition per use in `step1Misses` for one copy per build, `sharedHits` and `sharedMisses` for one copy per range), a `bumps` line (bump events and blocks by source between the previous use's own marks and this use, the first source and tag, driver stamps by tag, the bumps of the use's own marks (`own`; `cpuAtMarks` counts the uses whose own marks found CPU stores, a miss because a collect at the use would have stamped them), labels and flips between uses and the uses with neither, flush-hook reads by read site, address-based builds over it), an `images` line (image write-backs over the region since the previous use by flush reason, with their bytes into a unit-shadow slab, into the import and through the CPU and the blocks kept for the CPU; image uploads over it by upload reason with the last stamper of their blocks) and a `lines` line (changed-copy runs, counted in the period of the run, and their samples, counted in the period their batch completed in: 128-byte lines that differ between the staging buffer and its snapshot, lines that do not, changed dwords, and `unwritten` samples whose readback the GPU never wrote, which are left out); then `flush` lines by flush reason (flushes that found pending images and those images, slab bytes the flush retiled and units it published right after), `write-backs` lines by reason, and a `drops` line (pending units a refresh dropped, by the drop rule's cause: new keys that are a clear code (`clearKeys`), a byte compare (`compared`) or a stamp (`stamped`); for the stamp drops alone, whether the newest stamp over the unit is newer than the generation at which its results became pending (`stampAfterPending`), not newer (`stampNotAfterPending`: results dropped for stamps no newer than they are), or the unit has no such generation (`stampNoPendingSince`), and in `stampNotAfterUnit` the stamp drops whose newest stamp is no newer than the unit's own generation). At flips a `copies` line counts the cached storage images holding a CPU copy of their guest bytes by what the copy holds, watch, import, pending results, idleness and the path that allocated it, with `release` giving the state of APS5_STORAGE_COPY_RELEASE, `released` the images whose copy is released and `keptToVerify` the released copies that APS5_VERIFY_STORAGE_COPY_RELEASE keeps aside. Nothing is collected for the census: a CPU store that no collect saw yet counts in the interval in which a later collect stamps it. Set and not 0 turns it on. Read once. |

At most 256 samples wait at a time; 0 records none. Read once. |

Queueing needs the release thread (it is off with APS5_RELEASE_UNDER_LOCK or APS5_RELEASE_ON_UNLOCK and before the first hand-off); without it, over the bound (APS5_SPREAD_DESTRUCTION_MAX_MIB) or when a list cannot grow, the caller destroys as before. A pending allocation is no longer retained and not yet free: the trace ledger (APS5_TRACE_MEMORY_BUDGET) counts it active, a whole allocation under the category `pending destruction`. An allocation failure (trim) and pool destruction destroy that pool's pending allocations at once (not the queued class owners), and device teardown destroys everything pending. With APS5_PIPELINE_STATS a `[spread-destruction]` line every 10 s gives the allocations queued, destroyed in portions, destroyed by the caller over the bound, destroyed by drains and pending (with MiB), the owners handed to the reaper (each class store batch also carries its emptied map), released in portions, by the caller and by drains, and for the window the longest portion and the largest pending list. Exact: only when and on which thread objects are destroyed changes; nothing that reaches the GPU does. Safe to flip at any time: the direct stores are kept, and what is pending is destroyed either way. Runtime switch. |

the worker merges a build when the finisher ring retires its job, sharing a stage plan with the same layout, key, compiled variant, push offset, vertex fetch layout and registered image views, and replacing one that has the same layout and key but differs in those; a shape's plans go with its shape record (a new shape, an eviction, a switch copy change) and with a verify difference, which refuses the shape's plans until its shape record is rebuilt; sets of shapes unused for 3 presents are swept when a build would pass APS5_DRAW_RECORD_MIB, and a set is freed only once the finisher front passes every job that carried it; the `[stage-plans]` line of APS5_PIPELINE_STATS counts, every 10 s and at each A/B phase event, the draws, the hits with the hits on today's path (the verified ones), input refetches, relocated hits, relocated base and value words and moved buffer bases patched, the misses by reason (no plans, refused, generation moved, user words unreadable, new layout, runs unreadable, new user words, new low bits, new run words, new combination, image views moved, content differs, inaccessible, queued label, flushing image, forget serial moved, publish moved, pending storage moved, device images, moved buffer fields, vertex layout differs, memory changed, fetch error), the draws that ran without plans (today's draw, indirect, lean front verify, inline), the builds, the builds deferred behind the shape's build in flight, and why a build gave no plan (not queued, generation moved, shape, indirect, stage count, geometry back end, capture, direct stage, program, result, patched programs, layout), the builds dropped and why (without an answer slot, as stale, for a replaced shape, for a refused shape, over the byte cap), the stage plans added, shared, replaced and evicted, the layouts and draw plans added, the draw plans known and evicted, the sets swept and cleared, the mode changes and demotions, with the shapes, stage plans, draw plans and bytes held; the committer half of a planned draw (a hit the finisher handed to it, whatever its identity: index buffer, counts, first vertex and first instance are the draw's own, and its move ticket, pass access memo, per-batch keep and pass hold depend only on the template, the targets and the moved ranges) proves its template as APS5_CLEAN_CURRENCY does without that switch, unless APS5_NO_PLAN_CLEAN_PROOFS is set: the proof is skipped while no clean range was dropped and the serials hold, a dropped clean range costs a clean-range query and a tracker stamp check, and anything else runs the full proof, while the targets' pass hold, the moved read-only buffers' pending-write checks and the buffer alias checks stay per draw; the `[stage-plans]` line then also counts, for the committer half of planned draws, the template proofs (skipped clean, skipped after a clean re-query, verified, verify differed, or run and why: first proof, last proof failed, not reusable, storage images, depth copies, own writes, not all clean, views moved, serials moved, a range forgotten, rewritten since the record, pending writes, written during the last proof, proved in full with clean proofs off), the targets (held, or refreshed after the first draw, in another pass, after a moved collect epoch, proof serial, pending serial or depth serials, or for a changed target) and the moved read-only buffers checked and trusted by the move ticket; | `APS5_STORAGE_COPY_RELEASE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/RuntimeSwitches.cpp` |  | at each flip, under the GPU mutex, a cached storage image idle for APS5_STORAGE_COPY_IDLE_FRAMES presents gives back its CPU copy of the guest bytes (`original`, and a `generationBaseline` buffer that holds no baseline) and keeps its GPU image; at most 64 live images are examined and 16 released per flip, and once 16 MiB were hashed at a flip the further images holding a copy wait for a later one (the first image is released whatever its size). With APS5_PIPELINE_STATS a `[storage-copies]` line every 10 s gives the cached images, those holding a copy and its MiB, those with their copy released, the copies released and read again in the window with their MiB, the units read again as changed and of them those read again outside a refresh (expected 0), and the idle images not released because results were pending; the first four reads outside a refresh that found a change also print a `[storage-copies]` line. Runtime switch: turned off, no copy is released, and released copies are still read again at their next use. |

Use a local filesystem path: the presenter checks file metadata under the trace mutex, so slow or network metadata access can stall presentation. Checks occur on presents at most once per 250 ms while waiting; an existing file opens at the original lower bound. The file is not removed, read, or checked again after opening. a submit at the batch cap after the runtime switch generation moved since the gate copied the cap, a gate check where today's flush decided nothing as early, and a register list overlap verdict that moved between the finisher check and the committer check count as by design); the draw finisher checks at the start and the end of each draw job that the lean segment slot the job reads was not taken by another segment meanwhile; every N-th lean draw runs today's draw, which is what executes, and compares the shape record's precheck verdict, the exact draw key and shape key, the relocation identity and alias key, the job's user words with the queue's registers, the shape's decode with the draw's user words against a fresh decode, the write template against today's held writes, the job's draw parameters, PA_CL_VS_OUT_CNTL and held key against today's hand-off, the relocation patches and donor the finisher's relocation with the job's user words finds for the alias against today's relocation with the queue's registers, both run at the check, the data entry's decode refreshed the finisher's way with the job's user words against today's refresh of it, and the device the lean job would use against today's; a draw whose held writes today's path leaves unknown while the shape knows them counts as by design, a differing verdict is no longer kept, a differing device is only reported, and a shape with any other difference takes today's draw until its record sees a new shader registry, the switch generation moves or the record is evicted; `[lean-front-verify] <item> <field> differs:` lines on stderr show the first eight differences of each 10 s window, and `[lean-front-verify]` lines count the checks per kind, the differences per field and the by-design cases every 10 s and at each A/B phase event; 1 checks every item; 0 or unset is off |

| Name | Kind | Source | Default | Description |

| `ANYPS5_AUDIO_IN_MUTE_ON_FOCUS_LOSS` | string | `core/libs/prx/libScePad/src/HostVolumeState.cpp` | 0 | `1` returns silence to the guest while the display window has no focus and resumes input when it returns; SDL capture stays open, and queued samples are cleared across focus changes; `0` or unset leaves input unchanged; any other value, including an empty one, stops the game with an error naming the variable |
| `ANYPS5_RUMBLE` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libScePad/include/PadRumbleIntensity.hpp` | 100 | host controller vibration strength as a whole percentage from 0 to 100, read once when the pad input starts and again by the audio-out controller sound card path; 100 or unset leaves vibration exactly as the title asks, 0 turns motor rumble, impulse-trigger rumble, controller-sound-card haptics and Bluetooth DualSense haptics off, in between scales the motor amplitudes with rounding and the haptic samples linearly; DualSense adaptive trigger effects are not scaled; any other value (empty, signed, fractional, above 100) stops the game with an error naming the variable |
| `ANYPS5_RUMBLE_ON_FOCUS_LOSS` | string | `core/libs/prx/libScePad/include/PadRumbleIntensity.hpp` | 1 | `1` stops the motor rumble and drops new pulses while the display window has no focus, sending one zero-intensity request when focus is lost and resuming with the next pulse when it returns; `0` keeps rumble on without focus; any other value, including an empty one, stops the game at start with an error naming the variable; the light bar and the pad state are not changed |
| `ANYPS5_PAD_IDENTIFY` | string | `core/libs/prx/libScePad/include/PadIdentify.hpp` | off | which controller-identification pulse to send when a controller is bound to a player slot: `off` sends none, `connect` sends the pattern once per binding (player N gets N short pulses), `always` also repeats it every 30 seconds while the title is not rumbling; the pulse is scaled by `ANYPS5_RUMBLE`, never overrides a title's rumble, and any other value (empty, uppercase, a list) stops the game with an error naming the variable |

| `APS5_PAD_REPLAY` | string | `core/libs/prx/libScePad/src/PadState.cpp` |  | path of an `APS5_PAD_RECORD` file whose states replace the buttons, sticks and analog triggers the title reads from controller port 0, timed from its first read; a file that cannot be read or is not a recording stops the title at that read with an error naming the variable |

| `APS5_OUTPUT_RESOLUTION` | string | `core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/VideoOutDriver.cpp` |  | `<width>x<height>` in pixels that replaces the host display's size when `sceVideoOutGetOutputStatus` and `sceVideoOutGetOutputStatusWithVrr` report the output resolution: 1 for a size below 3840 wide and 2160 high, 2 otherwise, as for the display; unset or empty follows the display, and any other value makes those calls throw |

| `APS5_NO_ARM_COMPARED` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libc/src/GuestArena.cpp` |  | on Windows, turns off compared-memory arming: pages the AGC driver returns to the write watch, and host imports that stay watched, are marked to be compared when shared, so a clearing collect over such a shared page fails (its readers compare bytes) and the page is not armed, instead of being armed like other memory so a guest write faults |
| `APS5_NO_HOST_REGION_INDEX` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libc/src/GuestArena.cpp` |  | tests a range against every host region of the arena interval in turn instead of searching the sorted, merged region index built at startup, on Windows |

| `APS5_NO_WRITE_WATCH` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libc/src/GuestArena.cpp` |  | disables write watching so guest buffers are fully re-read on every use |

| `APS5_TRACE_AFFINITY` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libc/include/CpuTopology.hpp` |  | prints a line naming the role, tid and mask of every thread pinning |
| `APS5_TRACE_CALLS` | string | `core/libs/prx/libc/src/specifics/x86_64/CallTrace.cpp` |  | comma-separated libraries (or all) whose guest calls are traced; an empty import-library label uses the defining host module filename when it can be found |
| `APS5_TRACE_CALLS_FILE` | string | `core/libs/prx/libc/src/specifics/x86_64/CallTrace.cpp` |  | file rewritten every 10 seconds with each traced import called so far and its number of calls |
| `APS5_TRACE_EXIT` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libc/src/Process.cpp` |  | prints a backtrace of the caller of exit, _Exit and the process-exit path |
| `APS5_TRACE_SSE4A` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libc/src/specifics/windows/CrashReport.cpp` |  | prints each emulated SSE4a instruction once per rip |

| `APS5_VERIFY_RESIDENT_FIRST` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libc/include/WindowsMappings.hpp` |  | with APS5_RESIDENT_FIRST_COLLECT on, also looks up every collect cursor in the 16 KiB views map and throws when the resident-first answer differs |
| `APS5_WATCH_ADDR` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libc/src/specifics/windows/CrashReport.cpp` |  | absolute hex address whose writes are reported after its page is committed |
| `APS5_WATCH_WRITE` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libc/src/specifics/windows/CrashReport.cpp` |  | guest ELF vaddr whose 8-byte writes are reported by single-stepping the protected page |
| `APS5_WRITE_WATCH_FAULT_AHEAD` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libc/src/GuestWriteWatch.cpp` | 32 | on macOS, at most this many pages, up to the end of the faulting page's 64 KiB block, that a write fault unprotects and records as written when the two pages before it were written since the last collection; 0 or 1 unprotects only the faulting page |

### libkernel

| Name | Kind | Source | Default | Description |
|------|------|--------|---------|-------------|
| `ANYPS5_LOADEXEC_ARGV_SKIP` | string | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/Process.cpp` |  | on Windows, set to `1` to drop the first process argument and clear the variable |
| `APS5_COARSE_TIMED_WAITS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Time/TimedWait.cpp` |  | on Windows, rounds timed waits up to the scheduler tick |
| `APS5_DIRECT_MEMORY_FROM_FLEXIBLE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/DirectMemory/Export.cpp` |  | reports 12736 MiB less the configured flexible-memory size as the direct-memory size, and stops direct-memory allocation there, instead of 13824 MiB |
| `APS5_GAME_MODE` | string | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/HostPerformanceProcess.cpp` | off | asks the host for its game or performance mode while the title runs, read when libkernel loads: `off`, `on` or `auto`; on Linux it requests Feral GameMode (`libgamemode.so.0`, loaded with dlopen) and on macOS it holds a user-initiated, latency-critical `NSProcessInfo` activity; `on` logs a missing library or backend once, `auto` stays silent about it, and a refused request is logged once in both; nothing here is fatal, and any other value stops the title with an error naming the variable |
| `APS5_JOB_AFFINITY` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Pthread/src/Thread.cpp` |  | on Windows, pins the title's job worker threads to the efficiency cores of a hybrid CPU |
| `APS5_JOB_AFFINITY_MASK` | integer | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Pthread/src/Thread.cpp` |  | on Windows, hex core mask that job worker threads are pinned to |
| `APS5_MIMALLOC` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/MimallocRestart.cpp` |  | makes the process allocate through mimalloc instead of the system allocator: on Windows the entry stub (`core/relinker/elfpatcher/src/windows/WindowsEntryStubBuilder.cpp`) loads `mimalloc.dll`, on macOS libkernel restarts the title with `libmimalloc.dylib` in `DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES` and on Linux with `libmimalloc.so` in `LD_PRELOAD`; on macOS and Linux both variables stay set after the restart, so processes the title starts inherit them |
| `APS5_MIMALLOC_RESTARTED` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/MimallocRestart.cpp` |  | on macOS and Linux, set by libkernel for the restart that loads mimalloc and removed at start-up, so a restart that did not load it is not repeated |
| `APS5_NO_DIRECT_MEMORY_SECTION_POOL` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/DirectMemory/DirectMemory.cpp` |  | on Windows, creates a section for every direct-memory allocation and closes it at release instead of reusing released sections that never held guest data |
| `APS5_NO_JOB_AFFINITY` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Pthread/src/Thread.cpp` |  | on Windows, disables pinning of job worker threads |
| `APS5_NO_SLEEP_WAKE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Time/SleepWake.cpp` |  | keeps every sleep of 1 ms or less in full instead of ending it at a GPU batch, batch completion action or flip completion |
| `APS5_NO_TIMER_RESOLUTION` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Time/Time.cpp` |  | keeps the default 15.6 ms scheduler tick instead of raising it to 0.5 ms on Windows |
| `APS5_TIME_SCALE` | string | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Time/Time.cpp` | 1 | factor applied to the guest monotonic clocks and TSC; sleeps and host JobManager worker spin intervals still take real time; fractional factors work, and a value that is not above 0 uses 1 |
| `APS5_TRACE_APR` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Apr/src/Apr.cpp` |  | logs every APR file read with its path, offset, size and destination |
| `APS5_TRACE_EQUEUE` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Equeue/Equeue.cpp` |  | logs when an equeue drops the oldest pending events past its 64-event cap, rate-limited to the first drop and each power-of-two of the per-queue cumulative total |
| `APS5_TRACE_MEMORY` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/DirectMemory/DirectMemory.cpp` |  | logs direct-memory map, remap, protect, unmap and physical allocation operations |
| `APS5_TRACE_SYNC` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/EventFlag/src/EventFlag.cpp` |  | logs event-flag and semaphore operations |
| `APS5_TRACE_TIMERS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Time/Time.cpp` |  | on Windows, runs a timed-wait selftest at startup and prints mean, p50 and max latencies |
| `APS5_TRACE_USLEEP` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Time/Time.cpp` |  | reports every 2000 `sceKernelUsleep` calls the call sites that sleep most, including calls through `sceNetUsleep` and the `APS5_TRACE_TIMERS` measurement on Windows; guest `usleep` calls are not counted |
| `APS5_TRACE_WAITS` | presence | `core/libs/prx/libkernel/Time/Time.cpp` |  | aggregates blocking waits per guest call site and prints the longest-waiting sites every 3 s |
